# Modern UI Style2 Icon Catalog

Source asset: Assets/modernuserinterface-win/16x16/Modern_UI_Style_2.png
Review contact sheet: docs/art/modern-ui-style2-contact-sheet-4x.png

Grid: 34 rows x 49 columns, 1666 total 16x16 sprites.

Confidence counts: confirmed=465, probable=39, unknown=1162.

Unknown policy: unclassified entries keep the pattern unknown.r{row}.c{column}; the requested placeholder form unknown.r{row}.c{column} is preserved here so the catalog remains exhaustive without pretending uncertain icons are identified.
